- Drepane and Drepanum：德瑞帕涅與德列帕農
- Dulichium, the most important of the Odyssean islands：杜利基昂島，《奧德賽》諸島中最重要的一座
- ELPENOR, and Ulysses in Hades; his strange fall：厄爾佩諾爾，與奧德修斯在冥府；他奇怪的墜亡
- Elymi, Thucydides on the：埃利米人，修昔底德論
- Epic cycle, the Trojan books of the, known to the writer of the *Odyssey*：史詩集成，其中的特洛伊諸卷，《奧德賽》作者是知道的
- Eryx the Sican city on the top of, not abandoned：埃律克斯，山頂上的西卡尼城市，並未廢棄
- Eteoneus, only a char-butler：埃特俄紐斯，只是個打零工的鐘點管家
- Ethiopians, the, known as stretching all across Africa：埃塞俄比亞人，已知橫亙整個非洲
- Eubœa, assumed by Alcinous to be more distant from Scheria than Ithaca：埃維亞島，阿爾基諾俄斯以為它離斯刻里亞比伊薩卡還遠
- Eumæus, a male writer would have killed him; a native of Syracuse; perhaps a Greek：歐邁俄斯，男作者早就把他殺了；敘拉古人；也許是希臘人
- Eurybates, why hunched in the shoulders：歐律巴特斯，為什麼雙肩拱起
- Euryclea, becomes Eurynome; the price paid for her, a rejoinder to the *Iliad*; and Eurynome the same person：歐律克蕾亞，變成了歐律諾墨；買她所付的價錢，是對《伊里亞德》的回應；與歐律諾墨是同一個人
- Eurymachus, his death throes, and the good meat that was spoiled：歐律馬科斯，他的垂死掙扎，以及被糟蹋了的好肉
- Eurymedon, his overthrow：歐律墨東，他的覆滅
- Eurynome, *see* Euryclea：歐律諾墨，見歐律克蕾亞
- Ewes, and lambs, the present practice in Sicily：母羊，與小羊，西西里現今的做法
- FAVOGNANA, derived from Favonius; why Ulysses was not allowed to see：法維尼亞納，得名自法沃尼烏斯；為什麼不讓奧德修斯看見
- Fielding, his journey to the next world; on Homer：菲爾丁，他的《來世之旅》；論荷馬
- Fifths and Octaves, consecutive, forbidden：五度與八度，平行的，被禁止
- Four main lines of the argument：論證的四條主線
- Freeman, Prof., his map of the West coast of Sicily; visited Trapani：弗里曼教授，他的西西里西岸地圖；到過特拉帕尼
- GEESE, Penelope's dream about the：鵝，潘妮洛碧夢見的
- Genius, an offence, &c.; to be stamped out while young：天才，是一種冒犯等等；應趁年輕時撲滅
- Giacalone-Patti, Prof.：賈卡洛內－帕蒂教授
- Gladstone, the Right Hon. W. E., his canons as regards the text of *Iliad* and *Odyssey*; the "systematic and comprehensive" study of Homer still young; contrasts the *Iliad* and *Odyssey*; on Clytemnestra; on the time when Homer wrote：格萊斯頓，W．E．格萊斯頓閣下，他關於《伊里亞德》與《奧德賽》文本的準則；對荷馬「系統而全面」的研究仍在起步；對比《伊里亞德》與《奧德賽》；論克呂泰涅斯特拉；論荷馬寫作的年代
- Grammerton and Shrewsbury：格拉默頓與什魯斯伯里
- Greatheart, Mr.：大勇先生
- *Grotta del Toro*, the：公牛洞
- HADES, the writer's attitude towards women, in：冥府，作者在其中對女人的態度
- Harbour, Rheithron, used five times in the *Odyssey*; of Trapani, boatmen plying for hire：港口，瑞特隆，在《奧德賽》中用了五次；特拉帕尼的，攬客的船夫
- Hawk, tearing its prey, while still on the wing：鷹，在半空中撕裂獵物
- Helen, coming down to dinner at the house of Menelaus; mixes Nepenthe in the wine; outside the wooden house; her penitence for the wrong that Venus had done her; her present of a bridal dress to Telemachus：海倫，在墨涅拉俄斯家下樓用餐；在酒裡調入忘憂藥；在木屋外；為阿芙蘿黛蒂加在她身上的過錯懺悔；送給忒勒馬科斯一件新娘禮服
- Heraclidæ, return of, undateable：赫拉克勒斯後裔，其回歸，年代無法確定
- Hermione, her marriage found more interesting than that of Megapenthes; her marriage interpolated：赫爾米俄涅，她的婚事被認為比墨伽彭忒斯的更有趣；她的婚事是竄入的
- Hesiod, records a time when iron was not known：赫西俄德，記載了一個還不知有鐵的時代
- Homer, his infinite subtlety; the authoress of the *Odyssey* was angry with him; why the writer of the *Odyssey* let him so severely alone; protest against "Introductions to Homer," which include the *Odyssey*：荷馬，他無窮的精妙；《奧德賽》的女作者對他很生氣；為什麼《奧德賽》作者對他如此敬而遠之；抗議把《奧德賽》也包括進去的「荷馬導論」
- Horace, and *mediocribus esse poetis*：賀拉斯，與 mediocribus esse poetis（詩人的平庸）
- Horse the Trojan, story of the, shows that the Greeks did not know how Troy fell：木馬，特洛伊的，其故事顯示希臘人並不知道特洛伊是怎麼陷落的
- Hotel, man no use in a：旅館，男人在旅館裡毫無用處
- House of Ulysses, the：奧德修斯的宅邸
- Hypereia, near the Cyclopes; probable remains of its wall; not completely abandoned：許珀瑞亞，靠近庫克羅普斯人；其城牆可能的遺跡；並未完全廢棄
- IACENSES, the：伊阿森塞人
- Iakin, the coin, and the British Museum catalogue of Sicilian coins：IAKIN，錢幣，與大英博物館的西西里錢幣目錄
- Ιακὀς, means Ionian：Ιακὀς，意為愛奧尼亞的
- *Iliad*, catalogues of the; date of; the, refers to no event known to have been later than B.C. 1100：《伊里亞德》，其中的〈船表〉；其年代；未提及任何已知晚於西元前 1100 年的事件
- Ingroia, Cav. Prof. of Calatafimi：英格羅亞勳位騎士教授，卡拉塔菲米的
- Invention, not the authoress's strong point：虛構，不是女作者的強項
- Ionian Settlements on East Sicilian shores：愛奧尼亞人在西西里東岸的定居點
- Irus, and Iris：伊羅斯，與伊里斯
- Ismarus, and its wine：伊斯馬羅斯，及其酒
- Italia, and Œnotria：義大利，與俄諾特里亞
- Ithaca, drawn from Trapani and its neighbourhood; drawn from the island of Marettimo as well as from Trapani; "all highest up in the sea," sketch of：伊薩卡，取材自特拉帕尼及其一帶；取材自馬雷蒂莫島，也取材自特拉帕尼；「在海中位置最高」，其素描
- JEBB, Prof, the 1892 edition of his *Introduction to Homer*; his *Introduction to Homer*; his quotation from Bentley; on Bentley's not seeing that the *Odyssey* was of later date than the *Iliad*; on the house of Ulysses; and the date of the *Odyssey*; mentioned：傑布教授，他的《荷馬導論》1892 年版；他的《荷馬導論》；他引用本特利的話；論本特利沒看出《奧德賽》晚於《伊里亞德》；論奧德修斯的宅邸；與《奧德賽》的年代；提及
- Jews, their prayers, for men and for women：猶太人，他們的禱詞，男人的與女人的
- Jones, H. Festing; his, and the author's, joint oratorio Ulysses; mentioned：瓊斯，H．費斯廷．瓊斯；他與作者合寫的神劇《奧德修斯》；提及
- KIRCHHOF, on the first 87 lines of *Od*. i.：基希霍夫，論《奧德賽》卷一開頭 87 行
- LAERTES, why he left off calling on Penelope and coming to town; not poor：拉厄爾特斯，為什麼不再去探望潘妮洛碧、不再進城；並不窮
- Læstrygonians, derivation of the word, and *lastricare*; and Cyclopes one race; their relays of fresh milk：萊斯特律戈涅斯人，這個名稱的來源，與 lastricare（用石頭鋪路）；與庫克羅普斯人同屬一族；他們鮮奶的接力供應
- Lambs, living on two pulls a day at a milked ewe; and ewes—the present practice：小羊，靠每天吸兩口已擠過奶的母羊的奶過活；與母羊──現今的做法
- Lang, Mr. Andrew, on the house of Ulysses：朗，安德魯．朗先生，論奧德修斯的宅邸
- Latin names, the use of for Greek gods and heroes defended：拉丁名，為以拉丁名稱呼希臘神祇與英雄辯護
- Layard, Sir H., visited Trapani：雷亞德，H．雷亞德爵士，到過特拉帕尼
- List of points necessary for the identification of Scheria：認定斯刻里亞所需的要點清單
- Lubbock, Sir John, his hundred books：拉伯克，約翰．拉伯克爵士，他的百本好書
- Lucian, the most ungallant of all：琉善，最不懂得對女人殷勤的人
- MAGISTRATE, a hungry, Ulysses compared to：法官，一個飢腸轆轆的，奧德修斯被比作
- Malconsiglio, legends concerning：馬爾孔西利奧，關於它的傳說
- Malta, not Calypso's island：馬爾他，不是卡呂普索之島
- Man, and woman, never fully understand one another; can caricature each other, but not draw：男人，與女人，永遠無法完全了解對方；能互相漫畫化，卻不能互相描繪
- Marettimo, the island, had a wall all round it：馬雷蒂莫，該島，四周曾有城牆環繞
- Marseilles, the civic edition of *Iliad* and *Odyssey* used most largely by Aristarchus：馬賽，其城邦版《伊里亞德》與《奧德賽》，阿里斯塔克斯用得最多
- *Mediocribus esse poetis*, &c.：Mediocribus esse poetis（詩人的平庸）等等
- Megalithieism, the two kinds of：巨石建築，兩種
- Megapenthes, only married because his sister was：墨伽彭忒斯，只因為他的姊妹結婚才結婚
- Melanthius and the store-room：墨蘭提俄斯與庫房
- Menelaus, Ulysses. Alcinous, and Nestor, all from the same person; the collapse of his splendour in Book xv.; he used to sell wine; his frank *bourgeoisie*; his fussiness; why made to come back on the day of Ægisthus's funeral feast：墨涅拉俄斯、奧德修斯、阿爾基諾俄斯與涅斯托爾，都取材自同一個人；卷十五中他的排場垮了下來；他從前是賣酒的；他直率的小市民氣；他的瑣碎囉唆；為什麼安排他在埃癸斯托斯葬禮宴席當天回來
- Mentor, his name coined from Nestor's：門托爾，他的名字是從涅斯托爾的名字造出來的
- Milk rarely to be had fresh except in the morning in Sicily and S. Italy：鮮奶，在西西里與南義大利，除了早上很難買到
- Milking ewes, what Sicilian shepherds now do：擠母羊奶，西西里牧羊人現在的做法
- Minerva, not an easy person to recognise, and had deserted Ulysses for a long time; Ulysses upbraids her for not telling Telemachus about his return; her opinion of Penelope; her singular arrangements for Telemachus; Ulysses remonstrates with her; sending Telemachus a West wind to take him from Ithaca to Pylos; her total absence in Books ix.-xii. apologised for：雅典娜，不容易認出來，而且離棄奧德修斯很久了；奧德修斯責怪她沒把他回來的事告訴忒勒馬科斯；她對潘妮洛碧的看法；她替忒勒馬科斯做的奇特安排；奧德修斯向她抗議；送給忒勒馬科斯一陣西風，載他從伊薩卡到皮洛斯；她在卷九至卷十二完全缺席，詩中為此辯解
- Mixing-bowl, the, in an angle of the cloisters; Phemius lays his lyre down near the, and near the approach to the trap-door：調酒缸，在迴廊的一角；費彌俄斯把豎琴放在它附近，也就是通往暗門的路口附近
- Motya：莫提亞
- Mure, Colonel, on the Phæacian episode; visited Trapani：繆爾上校，論費埃克斯人的段落；到過特拉帕尼
- NARCISSUS, a cantata by H. Festing Jones, Esq., and the author：《納西瑟斯》，H．費斯廷．瓊斯先生與作者合寫的清唱劇
- Nausicaa, her dream, and going to the wash; her meeting with Ulysses; the ill-natured gossip of her fellow townspeople; her farewell to Ulysses; the most probable authoress：瑙西卡，她的夢，以及去洗衣；她與奧德修斯相遇；她的同城鄉親不懷好意的閒話；她向奧德修斯道別；最可能的女作者
- Nepenthe, the order in which its virtues are recorded：忘憂藥，其功效列舉的先後次序
- Neptune, turns the Phæacian ship into stone：波賽頓，把費埃克斯人的船變成石頭
- Nestor, Alcinous, Menelaus, and Ulysses, all drawn from the same person：涅斯托爾、阿爾基諾俄斯、墨涅拉俄斯與奧德修斯，都取材自同一個人
- OCCASIONAL notes, to show that the writer is a woman：零星札記，用以顯示作者是女人
- Octaves consecutive：八度，平行的
- *Odyssey*, the examples of feminine mistakes; refers to nothing of later date than B.C. 1100：《奧德賽》，女性錯誤的例子；未提及任何晚於西元前 1100 年的事
- ὀρσοθύρα, the; the way towards was in the corner of the cloister, near the mixing-bowl：ὀρσοθύρα（暗門）；通往它的路在迴廊一角，靠近調酒缸
- Œnotria, and Italia：俄諾特里亞，與義大利
- Olympia, apparently unknown to the writer：奧林匹亞，作者顯然不知道
- Orsi, Dr., mentioned; and pre-Corinthian cemeteries near Syracuse：奧爾西博士，提及；與敘拉古附近前科林斯時期的墓地
- Ortygia, and Syra：俄爾提基亞，與敘里埃
- PAGOTO, Signor Giuseppe：帕戈托，朱塞佩．帕戈托先生
- Pantellaria, rightly placed as regards Scheria; still a prison-island：潘泰萊里亞，相對於斯刻里亞的位置放得正確；至今仍是監獄島
- Parmenides, calls the moon Cyclops：巴門尼德，稱月亮為庫克羅普斯
- Penelope, her web; gets presents out of the suitors; scandalous [ver] of her conduct in ancient writers; versions she protests too much; did she ever try snubbing or boring; Minerva's opinion of her; and the upset bath; gloating over the luxury of woe; not a satisfactory guardian of the estate; tells her story to Ulysses before Ulysses tells his to her：潘妮洛碧，她的織物；從求婚者那裡撈到禮物；古代作家對她行為的醜聞版本；她的辯白過了頭；她是否試過冷落或煩死求婚者；雅典娜對她的看法；與打翻的洗腳盆；沉溺於悲傷的奢侈；不是家產稱職的看守人；在奧德修斯說出自己的故事之前，先向他講了她的故事
- Perseus, does not rescue Andromeda：珀爾修斯，沒有去救安德洛墨達
- Phæacian women, their skill in weaving, and general intelligence：費埃克斯婦女，她們的織藝與一般才智
- Phæacians, the, making drink offerings to Mercury (covert satire); Ulysses' farewell to the; a thin disguise for Phocæans; used 50-oared vessels like the Phocæans：費埃克斯人，向赫耳墨斯奠酒（暗中諷刺）；奧德修斯向他們道別；是福凱亞人的薄薄偽裝；和福凱亞人一樣使用五十槳的船
- Phemius, begs for mercy：費彌俄斯，求饒
- Phocæa; and Phocæans：福凱亞；與福凱亞人
- Phocæa, an Ionian city surrounded by Æolians：福凱亞，被埃俄利斯人包圍的一座愛奧尼亞城市
- Phocæans, the, used 50-oared vessels; and Phocians：福凱亞人，使用五十槳的船；與福基斯人
- Phœnician quarrymen's marks on walls of Eryx：腓尼基採石工人在埃律克斯城牆上留下的記號
- Phœnicians, the, distrusted, but not much known about Phœnicia：腓尼基人，不受信任，但對腓尼基所知不多
- Piacus：皮亞庫斯
- Pic-nic, a, to Polyphemus's cave：野餐，到波呂斐摩斯洞去
- Pisistratus, accompanies Telemachus to Sparta; does not like crying during dinner; gets no present：佩西斯特拉托斯，陪忒勒馬科斯到斯巴達；不喜歡在吃飯時哭；沒得到禮物
- Platt, Mr. Arthur, on the house of Ulysses：普拉特，亞瑟．普拉特先生，論奧德修斯的宅邸
- Poetesses, early Greek, abundant：女詩人，早期希臘的，為數眾多
- Policeman, identifying prisoner：警察，指認犯人
- Polyphemus, and his cave, drawn from life; his system of milking; his cave still called *la grotta diPolifemo*; the rocks he threw, Asinelli and Formiche; had two eyes; and Conturràno：波呂斐摩斯，與他的洞穴，取材自真實生活；他的擠奶方式；他的洞穴至今仍稱 la grotta di Polifemo（波呂斐摩斯洞）；他扔的岩石，阿西內利與福爾米凱；有兩隻眼睛；與孔圖拉諾
- Portazza, and Telepylus：波爾塔扎，與忒勒皮洛斯
- QUARRY, called Dacinoi：採石場，名叫達奇諾伊
- RAFT, Ulysses'：木筏，奧德修斯的
- Raven rock, the：科拉科斯岩（烏鴉岩）
- Rheithron, the harbour, used five times in *Odyssey*：瑞特隆港，在《奧德賽》中用了五次
- Rudder, the poetess's ideas about a：船舵，女詩人對它的想法
- "Ruler," a two foot, betraying a writer as a woman：「尺子」，兩英尺長的，洩露了作者是女人
- SALT works of S. Cusumano：聖庫蘇馬諾鹽場
- Sappho, and other early Greek poetesses：莎芙，與其他早期希臘女詩人
- Sardinian smile, a：薩丁尼亞式的冷笑
- Scheria, means Jutland; and Drepane, ancient names of Corfu：斯刻里亞，意為日德蘭；與德瑞帕涅，都是科孚的古名
- Schliemann, visited Trapani：施里曼，到過特拉帕尼
- Seals, the intolerable smell of; or Phocæ, malicious allusion to Phocæans：海豹，其難以忍受的臭味；或稱 Phocæ（海豹），是對福凱亞人的惡意影射
- Segesta, later than the *Odyssey*：塞傑斯塔，晚於《奧德賽》
- Selborne, Lord, his reminiscences：塞爾本勳爵，他的回憶錄
- Servants, like being told to eat and drink：僕人，喜歡被招呼吃喝
- Shelley, on the sweetness of the *Odyssey*：雪萊，論《奧德賽》的甜美
- Shield of Achilles, the, its genuineness defended：阿基里斯之盾，為其真實性辯護
- Shipwreck, and loss of Ulysses' ship：船難，與奧德修斯之船的損失
- Shirt, a clean, Alcinous' and his sons' views concerning：襯衫，乾淨的，阿爾基諾俄斯與他兒子們對此的看法
- Shrewsbury, and Grammerton：什魯斯伯里，與格拉默頓
- Sicels, in the *Odyssey*：西克洛伊人，在《奧德賽》中
- Σικανίης, not corrupted into, Σικλίης：Σικανίης，並未被訛寫為 Σικλίης
- Sirens, the, and Didyme：塞壬，與狄迪墨
- Sleep, the, of Ulysses：睡眠，奧德修斯的
- Smyth, Admiral, on the Æolian islands and on Charybdis：史邁思海軍上將，論埃俄利斯群島與卡律布狄斯
- Snow, frequent in the *Iliad*, but hardly ever named in the *Odyssey*：雪，在《伊里亞德》中常見，在《奧德賽》中卻幾乎沒提過
- Spadaro, Prof., of Marettimo：斯帕達羅教授，馬雷蒂莫的
- Sugameli, Signor：蘇加梅利先生
- Suitors, the, how many from each island; they are also the people who were sponging on Alcinous; they cannot be perfect lovers and perfect spongers at the same time; their version of Penelope's conduct：求婚者，各島各來了多少人；他們也就是那些在阿爾基諾俄斯家白吃白喝的人；他們不可能同時是完美的情人和完美的食客；他們對潘妮洛碧行為的說法
- Sun, turnings of the：太陽的轉向
- Sun-god, the, leaving his sheep and cattle in charge of two nymphs：太陽神赫利俄斯，把他的綿羊與牛交給兩位寧芙看管
- Swallow, Ulysses bowstring sings like a; Minerva flies out to the rafters like a：燕子，奧德修斯的弓弦唱得像燕子；雅典娜像燕子一樣飛上椽木
- Syracuse, pre-Corinthian：敘拉古，前科林斯時期的
- TARRAGONA, the walls of：塔拉戈納，其城牆
- Taygetus range, still roadless：塔宇革托斯山脈，至今仍無道路
- Tedesco, Signor, of Marettimo：泰德斯科先生，馬雷蒂莫的
- Telegony, the, and the *Odyssey*：《忒勒戈尼亞》，與《奧德賽》
- Telemachus, lectured by Minerva; and by Penelope; the two great evils that have fallen on his house; only twelve years old when Ulysses went to Hades; his alarm about his property; did not tip Eteoneus：忒勒馬科斯，被雅典娜教訓；也被潘妮洛碧教訓；降臨他家的兩大禍患；奧德修斯下冥府時他才十二歲；他為家產擔憂；沒給埃特俄紐斯小費
- Telepylus, a fictitious name：忒勒皮洛斯，虛構的名字
- Temesa, copper mines of; its people did not speak Greek：忒墨塞，其銅礦；當地人不說希臘語
- Tennyson, and the Arthurian legends：丁尼生，與亞瑟王傳說
- Theoclymenus sees the doom that overhangs the suitors and leaves the house; his presence in the poem, strange：忒俄克呂墨諾斯看見籠罩求婚者的厄運，便離開宅邸；他在詩中出現，很奇怪
- Thersites, and Eurybates：忒耳西忒斯，與歐律巴特斯
- Tholus, the：圓頂屋（tholus）
- Thucydides, and "Phocians of those from Troy"; on the Cyclopes and Læstrygonians; substantially in accord with the writer of the *Odyssey*; biassed in favour of the Corfu Drepane rather than the Sicilian Drepanum：修昔底德，與「來自特洛伊的那些福基斯人」；論庫克羅普斯人與萊斯特律戈涅斯人；與《奧德賽》作者大體一致；偏向科孚的德瑞帕涅，而非西西里的德列帕農
- Tiresias, his prophecy, and warning about the cattle of the Sun：忒瑞西阿斯，他的預言，以及關於赫利俄斯牛群的警告
- *Toro, grotta del*：公牛洞
- Trapdoor, the; the way towards was in the corner of the cloisters near the mixing bowl：暗門；通往它的路在迴廊一角，靠近調酒缸
- Trapani, what any rival site has got to show before claiming much consideration：特拉帕尼，任何競爭地點在要求重視之前必須拿出的東西
- Trapani and Ægadean islands from Mt. Eryx, sketch of：從埃律克斯山望見的特拉帕尼與埃加迪群島，素描
- Troy, date of its real or supposed fall：特洛伊，其真實或假想的陷落年代
- "ULYSSES," H. Festing Jones's and S. Butler's oratorio：《奧德修斯》，H．費斯廷．瓊斯與 S．巴特勒合寫的神劇
- Ulysses, fastens his chest with a knot that Circe had taught him; his deep sleep; upbraids Minerva for not telling Telemachus about his impending return; and Argus; rebukes Amphinomus; rebukes Eurymachus; he and Telemachus remove the armour; his brooch; having his feet washed by Euryclea; compared to a paunch cooking before a fire; his bedroom, surmise that the maids were hanged all round it; interview with Laertes in the garden; eating with Dolius; his farewell speeches to the Phæacians, and to Queen Arete; his main grievance a money one; he, Alcinous, Menelaus and Nestor, all drawn from the same person; always thankless; why not allowed to see either Favognana or the Scherian coast; house of, and that of Alcinous：奧德修斯，用喀耳刻教他的結把箱子捆好；他的沉睡；責怪雅典娜沒把他即將回來的事告訴忒勒馬科斯；與阿爾戈斯；斥責安菲諾摩斯；斥責歐律馬科斯；他與忒勒馬科斯搬走甲冑；他的胸針；讓歐律克蕾亞洗腳；被比作在火前烤著的羊肚；他的臥房，推測女僕們就吊死在它四周；在園中與拉厄爾特斯會面；與多利俄斯一同吃飯；他向費埃克斯人與阿瑞忒王后的告別辭；他主要的委屈是錢的問題；他、阿爾基諾俄斯、墨涅拉俄斯與涅斯托爾，都取材自同一個人；總是不知感恩；為什麼不讓他看見法維尼亞納或斯刻里亞海岸；他的宅邸，與阿爾基諾俄斯的宅邸
- Unconscious, examples of：無意識，其例
- Ustica, as the island of Æolus：烏斯蒂卡，作為埃俄羅斯之島
- VAULTED room, the：拱頂房間
- Virgil, and Æneas in Hades; gives the Cyclopes only one eye; and Drepanum：維吉爾，與冥府中的埃涅阿斯；讓庫克羅普斯人只有一隻眼睛；與德列帕農
- WALL, the Iliadic, date of：城牆，《伊里亞德》中的，其年代
- Wandering cliffs, the：漂移之岩
- Wolf, his theory baseless and mischievous：沃爾夫，他的理論毫無根據又貽害無窮
- Woman and man, never fully understand one another; can caricature each other, but not draw：女人與男人，永遠無法完全了解對方；能互相漫畫化，卻不能互相描繪
- Women, single, will not have a man in the house if they can help it; in Hades, the writer's attitude towards; treatment of the guilty, in the house of Ulysses：女人，單身的，只要能避免，就不讓男人住進家裡；在冥府中，作者對她們的態度；有罪者的處置，在奧德修斯家中
- World, its greatest men know little of the：世界，最偉大的人對它所知甚少
- YORK, the Duke of, and his marriage：約克公爵，及其婚事
- Young people, apt to be thoughtless：年輕人，容易思慮不周
- ZIMMERN, Miss Helen：齊默恩，海倫．齊默恩小姐
- Zummari, la Caletta dei：祖馬里，祖馬里小灣
